# Unfilter digest 07 — pregnancy, birth and the first weeks

Written 2026-11-17 for the rewrite of synthesis/findings.md. Scope: all 350 UR02, 435 UR05 and 378 UR11 quote records (1,163 total), and each ledger’s “What these posts add” section. The comparison is against the findings including its 2026-11-16 restoration; that version already recognizes illness, consent, difficult recovery, coercion gaps and reassurance loops.

Counts here count quote records, never people. The share was selected because material had been excluded; its subject balance cannot establish prevalence. Several records can come from one post or one continuing account. Full evidence fields below preserve spelling, punctuation, source obfuscation, markdown and uncertainty. Money excerpts are literal contiguous passages, with no inserted ellipses. Interpretations, request descriptions and recommendations for the rewrite are labeled by their context.

Clinical explanations, risk figures, diagnoses, medication effects, reporting fears and accounts of legal or service rules remain attributed testimony. No network research or independent clinical/legal verification was performed. No quoted account establishes that a guide changes a clinical outcome or that its author would pay for Mamivio.

Provenance to carry forward: the two crossposts behind [pbu02-0031] and [pbu02-0247] are one account. [pbu02-0081] is a public-health announcement. The shopping request [pbu05-0021] [pbu05-0022] and intimacy request [pbu05-0044] [pbu05-0045] carry historical possible-AI/template cautions. Food Recalls [pbu05-0206] [pbu05-0207] was flagged as a possible plug; neither accuracy nor commercial independence was established. [pbu05-0420] is an educational/promotional clinical case, not a parent’s independent treatment success. [pbu05-0372] [pbu05-0373] [pbu05-0374] [pbu05-0375] [pbu05-0376] are one retrospective. The chronology in [pbu11-0121] [pbu11-0122] [pbu11-0123] conflicts with its archive date, so it cannot establish event dates. [pbu11-0174] carries a historical possible-supplement-seeding note. These cautions qualify provenance; they do not remove the words.

## Patterns

### 1. Preparation can lead to a repeat scan, loss treatment, stillbirth or neonatal death rather than a continuous countdown to bringing a newborn home.

**Current findings section:** §2 The arc; §6 fears and asks; §10 postpartum preparation.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends.**

Current wording: “Make the first two weeks a full section: feeding, sleep, recovery supplies, the partner's shifts, visitors.”

**What should change / interpretation:** Keep that section, but replace the implied uninterrupted pregnancy-to-newborn arc with branches for uncertain viability, miscarriage management, birth after loss and bereavement after delivery. Include the practical questions parents actually ask about pain, telling existing children and trying again. Birth and postpartum also happen when a baby dies.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0033]**

> I had to stay the night and they explained to me that the chance that the sac healing itself when its such a big loss of water is very very small and that most likely the pregnancy will end with miscarriage. The two biggest possibilities are either the contractions spontaneously starting within the week or if not, the baby stays put and its heart beating but the sac does not repair itself and the baby will not be able to develop since theres no room for growth. With the latter, we have the options of either terminating the pregnancy or letting nature run it course but this could take multiple weeks.

**[pbu11-0034]**

> I'm exhausted of being anxious 24/7 and having to wait to for a train wreck to happen. The days seem so long and empty. Everyone's saying to not wait too long to try again after it's happened but no matter how much I want a baby, I just can't go through all of that fear again so soon. My first pregnancy has been purgatory and now I'll be reliving that fear every time. I don't know, I feel very lost.

**[pbu11-0208]**

> My daughter was alive. Nothing appeared wrong with her, except coming too early. Her legs and arms were moving, even her tiny lips were opening, I could feel her heartbeat. I desperately asked them to do something, but they said nothing is possible, her lungs are just not developed enough. She stayed in my arm until she passed away, about an hour after birth. 

**[pbu11-0361]**

> I delivered her vaginally after an induction that started that day, and my husband and I spent time with her before they took her body. We spoke with her and took some photos and videos to remember her. She was so beautiful, with my husband's eyes and my lips. They're now going to do further genetic testing and an autopsy, and we'll need to wait a couple of weeks for the results, and I hope there is a clear answer in there for us in those results, perhaps something that an amniocentesis could catch in the future. Currently feeling all of the heavy feels; it's been the hardest thing I've ever gone through and it still doesn't feel like I'm living "my" life.
> 
> It's been so hard for my husband also, as well as my mom that was in the room with us during delivery. I feel like they're broken. I know it's only four days in, and I know healing will take time.

**[pbu11-0362]**

> *One of my initial questions is - for those that experienced a similar loss (and carried your baby for a similar time), how long did you wait until you tried to conceive again? I'm almost 35 years old, so I'm mindful of that.* Our midwife said to wait a year to allow your uterus and cervix to recover completely. But I know women that have gotten pregnant after like 4 or 6 months and delivered healthy babies. I'm going to book an appointment with a high-risk OB to discuss as well. 

**[pbu11-0368]**

> My heart is broken. I wanted my son who is 2 to have a sibling. I am nervous about the d&c which won’t be for a few days. I’m cramping. I’m scared. I’m scared I won’t be able to have another baby. I’m scared of how anxious I’ll be during my next pregnancy. I have health ocd and anxiety already. I don’t know how to grieve. I don’t know how to say goodbye. My dreams and plans for the future are ruined. 
> 
> I’d love to hear stories from others who this has happened to. Preferably if you’ve had a great pregnancy and birth after. I’m so scared and feel so alone and I want to be there for my 2 year old but I also just want to be in the dark and cry. 

**[pbu02-0094]**

> I’m not sure what to expect from whatever procedure I’ll need for the miscarriage, and I honestly don’t have the mental capacity to start over, tracking every cycle, scheduling sex, trying even when we’re exhausted, and getting that negative test every month.

**[pbu02-0192]**

> I'm so frustrated that I always get this awful morning sickness that starts before 6 weeks. I'm so frustrated that I'm having to do this for a third time without knowing if I'll have my rainbow baby (my viability scan is in 2 weeks). I'm just so tired of this awful first trimester Groundhog Day.

**[pbu02-0329]**

> What’s really confusing me is that it’s now been almost 5 weeks since the pregnancy stopped developing, and I still haven’t passed anything or had any bleeding. My pregnancy tests are also still dye stealers and I’m feeling very pregnant.

**[pbu05-0099]**

> Back in November I had another MMC, when we found out at the 12 week scan we were so shocked and devastated we didn't think much and went with the medical route as it seemed most harmless. Unfortunately it was the most painful experience of my life, as I was told to just take paracetamol which didn't do anything for the pain.. I was silently prescribed a codeine-paracetamol pill too without any instructions around that. I couldn't even take that in the end, because by the time the pain was at its worst, I had already taken so much paracetamol (following nurse's instructions) that I would overdose if I did. My partner was calling EPU for advice all night but they weren't picking up.

**[pbu05-0100]**

> 1) if I decide to go with the medical route again , what pain management plan can I ask for, what are the strongest painkillers they can prescribe in the UK under these circumstances? I have read in the US they might prescribe some stronger opioids but I doubt here they do. I don't want to be told paracetamol again and stay silent but what can I realistically ask for?
> 
> 2) just today I have been considering the surgical route because I am so traumatised by my medical abortion experience. For me the only benefit of it is they might agree to examine the embryo, but in my case there is no embryo developed so nothing to reliably examine anyway. I have heard the risk of scarring is minimal if done using vacuum, so I plan to ask if this is how they do it. Anything else useful I can ask ?
> 
> I am really posting this because during my first MMC we were so unprepared and didn't ask anything and regret it. I would like to ask people in the UK who have been through this, what questions do you wish you asked at the appointment and in hindsight, what would you have done differently?

**[pbu05-0107]**

> We went for our first scan this morning, and unfortunately found out bub has no heartbeat. They were measuring up as being the right gestation (my dates work out to say I am about 13+2) so it looked like they very recently passed.
> 
> I have no physical symptoms yet and have an appointment tomorrow at the early pregnancy unit to discuss my options, but I’m nervous. I miscarried years ago at around 10 weeks and it was physically horrendous, so I’m really apprehensive about this time around. 
> 
> Any other ladies had any experience of this? I have no clue what to expect and I have 2 other children who will be home soon, one of which is 5 and I have to tell that the baby didn’t grow as we’d hoped. We’d pre warned her it might be the case but I’m dreading telling her all the same.

**[pbu05-0113]**

> They did the ultrasound and said they could see the sack but no signs of pregnancy. So wanted to do an internal scan to be sure. I was frightened, I just knew this wasn’t right. I cried as I put that gown on and undressed myself to prepare. 
> 
> Internal scan showed baby measuring at 8w3d. It should have been 11w3d. No heartbeat. I’ve had a missed miscarriage and my body is still now continuing on as if I am pregnant.   
> I had to make the walk back through the hospital and back to my car alone trying so hard not to breakdown.

**[pbu05-0208]**

> Fast forward at 9 weeks I got my 1st ultrasound , no heartbeat, the Dr made us wait 2 weeks saying it could have been a late ovulation/implantation. 11 weeks in and no heartbeat, missed miscarriage, gotta perform D&C on February 14th (ironic). 
> 
> It's 29th of April and gosh it gets worse. I got 2 periods after the surgery and my cycle is just a bit heavier but that's it.. im emotionally devastated. I cant help but remember how far along I would have been today.
> 
> On the 20th week mark, my brain woke me up with a panic attack. I dream of the surgery room/table often.

**[pbu05-0421]**

> We had a missed miscarriage at 17+3 in Feb this year our first pregnancy after 2 years of trying. We then found out we were pregnant again in June. 
> 
> I have been an anxious mess but so well supported, haven't had the easiest journey with a subchorionic hematoma but baby seemed to be growing ok and everything looked ok. 
> 
> We got to out 20 week scan on monday, told we were having a daughter, everything looked great but she was measuring a little small - they referred us to fetal medicine for extra precaution. Okay no reason to panic we thought, a little small can still be fine. 
> 
> Fetal medicine saw us tuesday - the day after. Unfortunately it looks like I have placental insufficiency and baby is measuring about 2 weeks behind. Doppler wasn't looking great either. Nothing we can do but watch and wait to see if she can keep going and become viable. They told us not to lose hope but I'm pretty sure her odds are small.
> 
> We are both so so sad, we so desperately want this baby and I'm really struggling that I can feel her wriggling about knowing she might pass at any moment. 
> 
> No real questions but just looking for support from people who may have gone through this.

### 2. Loss leaves bodily needs, reminders and a contested parental identity; grief has no single required intensity.

**Current findings section:** §2 first weeks; §3 what others say; §6 identity; a new loss/bereavement subsection.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends.**

Current wording: “The first weeks include incapacity, feeding pressure and delayed connection.”

**What should change / interpretation:** Add lactation after stillbirth, donation as a source of comfort, the loss of that comfort, due-date support, anniversaries and unwanted notifications. Retain opposite preferences about Mother's Day and different grief intensities rather than prescribing one acknowledgement or timetable.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0059]**

> Grief is not a hierarchy and our relationship should not be a competition about who has suffered more.

**[pbu11-0150]**

> Unless you count last year when everyone was wishing her happy Mother’s Day while she was pregnant, which we certainly don’t. Sidebar: did anyone else run into that during their first pregnancy? Super weird… we had a miscarriage in 2024, so people saying Happy Mother’s Day to her while pregnant in 2025 felt a little premature. This is what we’re counting as her first haha

**[pbu11-0209]**

> It's been 5 weeks since this happened. In the meantime, except of inflammation of placenta, they haven't told me anything else caused this. They couldn't determine what cause the inflammation or why I haven't feel absolutely nothing till the delivery. As per doctors, it just happened.  
> It is imposable to describe what I am going through right now. I am still trying to process that I held both my mom and my daughter dead in my arms in only few months. Pain is immeasurable, despair, complete loss of sense or identity. I am not a daughter anymore, but I am also not a mom. 

**[pbu11-0244]**

> My water broke (PPROM) last Sunday and I had to deliver to my baby on Wednesday morning. Needless to say this was my worst nightmare coming alive. It’s been almost a week and I truly don’t know how to feel. My husband has been my rock, I am off from work and at my parents’ home but I feel like a burden because they don’t let me do anything (I know its because they care for me), but idk how to grief and with just sitting at home, I feel trapped.
> 
> My husband takes me out on drives to change the scenery but I can’t seem to enjoy anything. This was our first pregnancy and my husband and I had so many plans for our baby and now it’s just gone. I’m grieving but also is my husband and he’s being so strong whereas I feel like I am a mess. 

**[pbu02-0048]**

> So I decided to do it and started the pumping right away. I rented the equipment and was going to get one myself for donation. The lactation consultant had taught me how to do it and gave me the contact information for the donation center. I had to wait two days.

**[pbu02-0049]**

> I spent many months finding the perfect medication because I do suffer from bipolar. I couldn’t change it. It felt like another loss. I now feel hopeless. I couldn’t do it anymore. I angrily packed the pump and told my mom to take it away from me. Pumping was the last comfort for me. Now I don’t know how else to handle the loss of my perfect baby.

**[pbu02-0061]**

> My cousin miscarried and the baby’s due date is tomorrow. She’s having a rough time. What can I send or bring over? I’ve never miscarried so I want to be sensitive to the situation. Thank you!

**[pbu02-0149]**

> I thought that because I'm now pregnant and in my third trimester, I could feel a little joy towards Mother's Day, but I don't.   I have such conflicting feelings towards it.  After my miscarriages, Mother's Day became such a depressing day for me.  I think what hurt the most was that I felt like I was a mom because I was pregnant  several times for a short time, but it went unacknowledged.  What hurt the most was that no one wished me a happy Mother's Day.

**[pbu02-0180]**

> I keep getting notifications from all of the pregnancy pages I’ve visited/joined already and it’s tearing me apart every time.

**[pbu02-0339]**

> I'm not very very sad, because the baby hasn't even had a heartbeat but now I'm kinda worried about getting pregnant that it will happen again. I started also taking prenatal vitamins and I want to keep taking them for the next pregnancy. I want to wait till January to start trying again.

**[pbu05-0017]**

> I (35F) am so sad & emotionally numb. I just miscarried at 14 weeks. It was my first pregnancy. It was unplanned and I would've been a single mum. But I still really wanted it. I was so excited. Now I'm devastated that the unthinkable has happened to me. Such an awful feeling (the grief).

**[pbu05-0209]**

> My sister in law just gave birth and I called her husband and everything, but I gushed into tears afterwards, my mother in law called me saying "hey your turn will come and you'll have a healthy pregnancy " and she means well and she's supportive but her words kill me..
> 
> We're supposed to travel to her next Friday, and im so heartbroken, I don't wanna appear jealous or mean or grumpy but I cant help myself but cry when I remember that 3 months of vomiting, pain, fatigue all ended in a 15 minute surgery WITHOUT A BABY. 
> 
> does someone have a similar experience? And please tell me how not to cry in front of the whole family when I go the baby shower (Friday, they said baby shower will be after the baby arrives) ?

**[pbu05-0387]**

> This has been incredibly painful. I know some people may say, “It was early,” but to me, this was already my baby. I had already started imagining the future, already loved this little life, and already hoped this pregnancy would be the one that stayed.  
> I’m sharing this because I know I’m not the only woman who has experienced bleeding, scary HCG numbers, waiting for test results, and the horrible uncertainty of not knowing what is happening inside your own body.  
> If you are going through something similar, please know that you are not alone. Don’t let anyone make you feel like your grief isn’t real just because the pregnancy was early.  
> I’m still waiting for answers, and I’m trying to take this one moment at a time.

**Further supporting IDs:** [pbu02-0023] [pbu02-0179] [pbu02-0241] [pbu02-0263] [pbu11-0165]

### 3. Parents use symptoms, blood, test lines, movements and appointments to check whether the baby is still alive, so feeling better can feel worse.

**Current findings section:** §2 early pregnancy; §6 waiting and reassurance.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends.**

Current wording: “Fear and reassurance have a mechanism.”

**What should change / interpretation:** Specify viability surveillance rather than only information overload: going to the toilet, disappearing nausea, the gap before movement, movements becoming stronger or weaker, and relief that lasts only until the next appointment. Knowledge that symptoms vary does not necessarily stop checking.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0001]**

> However, tonight my anxiety about potentially losing the pregnancy or something going wrong is at an all time high. I’m second guessing telling them now or ever. I know I’ll have to at some point, but I feel like it’ll put a lot of pressure on me to do everything right to prevent anything from happening and I do that to myself as it is. I get anxious any time I pee for fear that I’ll see blood and I’ll be miscarrying. We’ve wanted a child so badly and now that I am pregnant I can’t enjoy it for my racing thoughts. This sucks.

**[pbu11-0010]**

> I’m about 5w3d today, and I keep having anxiety and I keep testing… like up to 3 tests a day to make sure the line is getting darker. It has, but I’m still beyond anxious. I can’t talk about this with family, because we’re waiting to say anything until after our first OB appointment at the end of the month. I don’t want to talk about it with friends, because a lot of mine are going through similar paths. My next therapy appointment is next week, and I plan to bring it up then. But in the meantime, I feel like I’m going crazy. Is this normal??

**[pbu11-0152]**

> Even after we found out we were pregnant again, it was hard to feel excited. Loss changes you. Every scan felt like another hurdle. Every appointment brought relief, but only until the next one. I don’t think either of us really believed we’d be bringing a baby home until it actually happened.

**[pbu11-0192]**

> The norm that first trimester is a wild ride is crazy. I’m almost 11w and haven’t thrown up once, only had mild nausea during 6w-8w. Had my first ob scan at 10w and the doc said, “we couldn’t have hoped for a better scan”. But you see all these stories of people being vulnerable in their first trimester (so sorry if you are one of them, I was there during a previous pregnancy too) and can’t help but go crazy that something is definitely wrong with the baby. For reference, my last pregnancy, I had terrible nausea also but ended up miscarrying. However, this pregnancy seems to be very easy going so far and I’m so thankful for it BUT my mind is frigging me up.

**[pbu11-0264]**

> So the past few days...no nausea. No vomiting. And I know this is extremely common once you are out of the first trimester--things are stabilizing and I should be happy about it. But now the lack of symptoms has me feeling anxious. Sort of a no winning situation I guess. I have a friend who is pregnant, she is currently 27 weeks, and she said the anxiety will fade once the kicks start...but this in between time is going to be so rough...we saw baby less then a week ago for ultrasound and NIPT and they were doing good and that should be comforting but I didn't expect to feel so shaken when my symptoms went away. 

**[pbu11-0268]**

> Everything has gone good so far in my pregnancy except I have little to no movements at home to the point where I’m in day care daily and triage every weekend to monitor baby’s movements, getting scans and consultant reviews every 2 weeks, I don’t know if anyone else has experienced similar and can make me feel better, just keep going back to thinking something is wrong again.
> 
> Has anyone had similar and what was the outcome with baby ? Any different syndrome or illness.

**[pbu02-0035]**

> I know symptoms (or lack of symptoms) don't necessarily mean anything, but after three losses it's hard not to analyze every little thing. ❤️

**[pbu02-0138]**

> my oura ring shows my body temp is getting back to optimal and was the lowest it’s been my whole pregnancy at +0.1 above baseline when I checked today. I am terrified I’m missing something. I have been on thyroid meds since right before I ovulated because tsh was 4.99 and when I had blood drawn at my first ob appt it was down to 0.82. I’m so terrified the meds have overcorrected to hyper instead of hypo. my next scan isn’t for another 11 days when I go in at 10w1d for NIPT bloodwork.

**[pbu02-0142]**

> I am only 4 weeks in but ever since confirming 3 days ago I have had constant anxiety. I am constantly alternating between praying over the safety and wellbeing of my baby, and trying not to get to attach to protect myself from the potential heartbreak a miscarriage would cause. I know all I can do at this point is wait but every time I go to the toilet I’m scared to see blood. I’ve tried the pregnancy miscarriage calculator but I’m just anxious.

**[pbu02-0240]**

> However, I have been starting to obsess over her movements lately and I’m constantly anxious questioning myself “did I feel her enough today”? “Was her movements stronger yesterday?” Or “at this time yesterday she moved a lot and today not so much”. It’s really starting to affect my mood and I just can’t calm down. I’ve already been to triage 2 times because I hadn’t felt her for awhile but of course she started wiggling the second I arrived and everything looked absolutely fine. Do I just accept that this is how life is with a child? The constant worry.

**[pbu02-0336]**

> I am so very very scared he will tighten his knot and axphixiate in utero or during labor. How am I supposed to live every day until delivery? When he's moving in scared he's making it worse. When he's not moving I'm scared hes gone.

**[pbu05-0188]**

> My 4th pregnancy in 14 months. Previous 3 were miscarriages… my clear blue is telling me 2-3 wks since conception and I’m around 5 wks … should it not showing 3+ wks now?! I’m so worried….

**[pbu05-0264]**

> yesterday i went to have first viability scan  
> ny LPM im 6w5d (ovulation was tracked vua ClearBlue LH surge test, LH surge showed peak May 4 so ovulation would be top may 5 latest)
> 
> they saw gestation sac but measuring only 9mm (more like 5w5d), yolk sac was present (4.4mm), the specialist did show me several times a tiny dot which he said was flickering, he said not heartbeat but bloodflow (!) and thats like the very beginning of the embryo
> 
> also first he said i must be earlier than 6w5d bit in report he says 6w5d (?!)
> 
> in the report however he wrote no embryo or fetal pole visualised, pregnancy of unknown viability told me to return in 14 days, he was like “all good for now no concerns!”
> 
> im of course very stressed and confused because
> 
> gestational sac of 9mm is def not for 6w5d, too small, since i tracked LH surge ..   
> whats the flickering dof with bloodstream he says beginning of embryo but then writes no embryo visualised
> 
> please share your experience on what to expect

**[pbu05-0273]**

> From about 6 weeks until 8+2, I was nauseous from morning until night. It was pretty miserable and I could barely function some days.
> 
> Then almost overnight at 8+3, the nausea disappeared. I’m now 8+5 and still waking up with no nausea. My breasts are still tender and I’m still extremely tired, but the nausea seems to have gone.
> 
> I know symptoms can come and go, but after a previous loss it’s hard not to worry. Has anyone had nausea disappear around 8 weeks and still gone on to have a healthy pregnancy?
> 
> Just looking for some positive experiences while I wait for my scan in 2 days.

**[pbu05-0357]**

> I’m currently 8+5 with my second pregnancy post-MMC in Apr-26. I had my first appointment 1 weeks ago ago - doctor said everything looked great (fetus measuring 8+0 and BPM > 160), but every day I feel more and more like history will repeat itself (doctor said everything looked great last time). My symptoms have been all over the place - some days I can’t move or keep food down and some days I feel 100% normal (like today). On normal days, I’m almost discouraged. Any other folks dealing with similar feelings / pregnancies? I wish I could just be positive and excited for a second chance, but I’m really struggling.

### 4. Scans, tests, stories and AI can reassure, fail to reassure or create a fresh problem; parents sometimes choose less checking.

**Current findings section:** §4 Google/AI/Reddit and apps; §6 reassurance; §11 trusted sources.

**Relationship to current conclusion: confirms and extends.**

Current wording: “Checking, apps, scans, family stories, media and chatbots may soothe or extend a loop; some people seek direct information and others seek less.”

**What should change / interpretation:** The restored conclusion holds. Make the contrast concrete: prior reassuring scans did not prevent a loss; a boutique and clinic disagree; written and verbal reports conflict; one parent declines more blood draws, another enjoys a Doppler, and another finds one increases anxiety. Some seek negative as well as positive outcomes. Do not turn this into one blanket rule about more information or fewer scans.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0252]**

> Hi guys, I’m not sure why I’m writing this but I guess I need reassurance. I found out I was pregnant a few days ago and I’m now 4 weeks pregnant. I have cramps and sore boobs. I went in for a scan today even though it’s early but it was because I had a miscarriage in December so I guess the doc wanted me to come in anyway to rule stuff out. But of course, we saw nothing when she did vaginal ultrasound. I was hoping to see maybe idk a small sac or something but nope. Now I’m extra anxious… I get paranoid when my boobs aren’t as sore as the first time or why I’m having more cramps than the first time too. 
> 
> I guess I just wanna know if anyone ever went in to checkup at 4 weeks and saw nothing but still ended up having a healthy pregnancy? 

**[pbu11-0285]**

> Anyone else newly pregnant after a loss/struggling with infertility, and taking pregnancy tests just because? I had a MMC last August and have been struggling with infertility for a year. Seeing negative after negative was so detrimental to my mental health that seeing my positive tests (no matter how faint) is the best feeling ever. 

**[pbu11-0321]**

> I’m 11w4d w mo/di twins. Haven’t seen them in a few weeks so decided to book a private ultrasound. BAD IDEA. The woman told me she was concerned one of the twins has a cystic hygroma. Of course my appt was on Friday at 4p, and my OB can’t get me in for another 6 days 😭 when I put the images into chat, it says it just looks like amniotic fluid, not a CH. how do I deal with this uncertainty for 6 more days? Has anyone had an experience w bad news at a boutique and they were wrong?

**[pbu11-0351]**

> Guys. I'm 13 weeks in a couple of days and I just have to say it does get better. I didn't think it would but I'm feeling more like myself and my energy is somewhat better (still loving a nap) we got our NIPT test back this morning and all low risk and a BABY BOY!! I'm feeling like I can be excited now. And I'm ready to announce. Hang in there this is such a wild ride. 

**[pbu11-0352]**

> After an ectopic, a chemical, and a year of unexplained infertility, we finally have a baby with a heartbeat in the uterus!
> 
> My husband is more relieved than I am as we’re only 6 weeks and so much can still happen. But for now, I’m taking in the best news we could have possibly gotten. My fears aren’t totally assuaged but at least I know for now my life isn’t in danger and I won’t have to go through a miserable treatment. 

**[pbu11-0377]**

> I had a TW// miscarriage 3 years ago and we’ve been struggling to get pregnant since, now I’m 6 weeks 2 days I had my very first scan ever on Sunday, I was a nervous wreck the whole weekend and on Sunday I was just even worse couldn’t stop crying was shaking and I felt bad but I couldn’t wait for the scan to be over with, we get to window to the womb and my nerves have settled a little I go into the scan room it was transvaginal which I’m used to due to having PCOS/Endo the women then said “there’s your baby” me and my husband burst out crying we got to see their wee heartbeat a very strong heartbeat🥺 measurements are 5.1mm which the woman said is perfect for how many weeks I am, I’m so excited I just keep looking at the scan crying I just feeling so blessed 

**[pbu02-0020]**

> Opted for a D&C, which in retrospect not sure I should have done. Anyway the Gemini is saying I need to get tested for a bunch of things before trying again. I have 1 living child who is 2. I just got my period and my OB gave me the green light to try again. I want another baby so bad, but I’m so worried about another loss.

**[pbu02-0197]**

> Based on everything I’ve found via AI, Gemini, chat GPT, (I know I’m an idiot for trusting AI but I’m at a loss) should I be worried? Anyone that can help me out I’d be extremely grateful. Stuck in limbo and afraid for bad news.

**[pbu02-0228]**

> I know all of these put me at a high risk of a miscarriage, but has anyone had a similar experience that they’re willing to share? I know it may not be positive, but I’m trying to mentally prepare for the worst while hoping for the best.

**[pbu02-0258]**

> Just got back from the OB and they said that my baby's heart rate was at 55bpm.. I went to a boutique last week and they said it was at 120bpm and we saw the heart beating on the screen... my doctor said not to trust places like that as they're not professional techs... she also said it is a 50/50 this will end in a miscarriage and to come back in 7-10 day to repeat the scan. My appt is in 9 days, is there any hope? Anyone have any success stories I've been crying a lot already and trying so hard to stay calm.

**[pbu02-0265]**

> I’m still struggling to wrap my head around the fact that it’s alive and growing and has a heart already chugging away. I was so certain my body was trying to miscarry because of the amount of pain I was in. But nope, healthy and growing grape just showing off in there. I go back the end of August to really see it properly this time since it’ll be big enough to really see then and now I’m so much less anxious.

**[pbu02-0272]**

> I am not too worried about miscarriage or something bad because I know the disappearance of symptoms don't always mean possible loss. 
> 
> I guess my question is - did anyone experience nausea/morning sickness that fluctuates throughout the week? Did it turn into more constant nausea later?

**[pbu05-0181]**

> Guys we’re been through an excruciating time after seeing this photo at our 10 week scan.
> 
> The doctor didn’t say anything is wrong but she asked my age (35) and sent me straight to the lab to have my blood drawn for a NIPT test 
> 
> In Germany your age alone isn’t enough to get the NIPT test covered by insurance, there usually has to be another sort marker for Down’s syndrome 
> 
> I showed it to a friend who is an OBGYN nurse and she said “that does not look normal” PANIC washed over me.
> 
> I saw a post on Reddit with a photo IDENTICAL to ours, crying about a “high NT at 10+3”
> 
> I showed our photo to another two friends who are actual OBGYN doctors and they both said the same thing:
> 
> \- 10 weeks is too soon for an NT scan
> 
> \- what you’re looking at in this photo might actually just be the amniotic sac

**[pbu05-0182]**

> My husband is a surgeon so he took me to his office and did another ultrasound on me around 12 weeks just to see.
> 
> Low and behold… it wasn’t the NT but rather the amniotic sac we saw in the ultrasound photo 
> 
> And the NT is totally perfectly normal 
> 
> NIPT test came back all clear 
> 
> I’m telling you this so you do NOT draw your own conclusions if your scan looks like ours. 
> 
> Everything is probably fine.

**[pbu05-0237]**

> Girls, if you have been pregnant before and miscarriage at 6 weeks AFTER a 6 week scan showing everything is fine and a flicker for heart beat and then a few days later misscarriage happens.   
> Now pregnant again would you go back for early scan?? 
> 
> \*\*it was a transvaginal scan first\*\*

**[pbu05-0239]**

> I was going to go but then I thought about it and I declined… 
> 
> From everything I’ve read, my HCG is rising appropriately and waiting for another set of results is just going to fuel my already amped up anxiety. 
> 
> I’m going to keep obsessing over the numbers instead of waiting for my first ultrasound. 
> 
> Plus, I want to change OB’s anyways… 
> 
> My husband told me it’s my choice but he still wants me to get another draw. 
> 
> I just honestly feel like at this point it’s not necessary.

**[pbu05-0252]**

> I know at home dopplers can be controversial, but I purchased one back during my first pregnancy. Unfortunately I lost the baby before I was able to use it, but this pregnancy I'm far along enough to try again at 12 weeks.
> 
> I use it with the idea in mind that even if I can't find the heartbeat, it's just something to do for fun and feel a little closer to my peanut while I wait to meet them. With a bit of searching, I was to hear that "galloping" like heartbeat and feel closer to my baby for a few minutes 🥰🥰🥰 It made my heart happy enough that I wanted to share with the other mom's on this sub.

**[pbu05-0382]**

> My dating ultrasound was originally scheduled for September 30, but I was having intense pain on the left side that required me to be seen. The hospital opted to perform an ultrasound, and then decided afterward that my September 30 ultrasound should be cancelled. They said everything was fine, but never determined the source of the pain. I will not have another ultrasound until around week 20. 
> 
> I think it’s also important to note that the radiologist noted I have a bicornuate uterus. My provider told me not to believe this portion of the report since I already have a living child and have had ultrasounds, sonohyserograms and hysterosalpingograms in the past. He said a bicornuate uterus would’ve been documented long before now. So if I’m not supposed to believe this one thing, how am I supposed to believe any part of the ultrasound report?

**[pbu05-0401]**

> I’m usually a commenter but I just thought back to when I was pregnant and searching for answers about anyone else who has experienced a baby with irregular (Ectopic) heartbeats. Like a lot of people I purchased a dopler ( I know wasn’t recommended) but this was my 7th pregnancy I had had 6 miscarriages before and wanted to hear him as soon as I possibly could without having to go to for a midwife appointment or a scan, this also gave me anxiety. I could never look at the screen. 
> 
> Just before my standard 28 week scan I picked up the beat myself, it went in a pattern of 3 instead of a normal beat pattern. When they picked it up at a midwife appointment I wasn’t shocked but that everyone around me medically started to panic telling me I need to triage right now. 
> 
> I cried so much because the hospital I was originally with also hadn’t had much exposure or experience with a baby that had this type of heartbeat. This meant they constantly put me on a monitor for hours sometimes and with no food. Because of the beats, the monitor would never reach criteria. In the end through sheer anxiety and fear I changed myself to bigger heart specialist hospital in London.

**[pbu05-0402]**

> Once I was under thier care I felt way more relaxed they looked at everything and said that this can just happen sometimes. The baby’s heart shape and chambers looked fine it’s the electrical pulses that can irregulate. Having had that in depth scan at 32 weeks which I took myself and made happen (that I should have had at 28 weeks as soon as they picked it up but didn’t send me to the fetal cardio specialist) this is where I could finally calm down abit. They said that approaching the 40 week mark the beats would more than likely regulate and once born would go back to normal normal.
> 
> This is exactly what happened with my boy ! He was monitored the minute he came out and every 3 hours for the next 2 days and everytime the reassured me it was fine. I just wanted to share this with you as some hospitals don’t have any experience with this and it’s more common than you think. Stay strong and try to calm down I cried everyday for the remainder of my pregnancy because the midwife’s had no clue about the condition. All the best with your pregnancies my baby is now 3 months and very much healthy xxx

**[pbu05-0426]**

> Please talk me off a ledge because I do not want to ask ChatGPT. I’m 6w5d pregnant with my first sono on Thursday at 7w1d. I’ve had a couple bouts of light brown tinged wipes after peeing that happened in the morning, once about 5 days ago which stopped  and then yesterday it was slightly deeper in brown and this morning which was very dark brown. 
> 
> Last night the pinching sensation started happening in my lower abdomen where my uterus is. It’s a pinch then stops and then maybe 20 minutes will pass it will pinch again but has been on and off for a few hours starting last night. 
> 
> Terrified I’m having ectopic pregnancy, terrified I will miscarry. I’m trying to hold off so badly for Thursday to prove to myself that i have the mental willpower but I’m freaking out.

### 5. After loss, buying, celebrating, reading pregnancy material or even booking a scan may feel like inviting another loss.

**Current findings section:** §4 what they try; §6 overload and fear; §10 ordering preparation.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends.**

Current wording: “and say what to do now and what can wait”

**What should change / interpretation:** A week-based buy-now/hold-off schedule also needs readiness, outcome and loss history. Preparation can be deliberately deferred, not merely disorganized. Show the other side: gifts can make shopping feel possible again. Content and notifications need to respond when a pregnancy ends, rather than continuing the expected week sequence.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0051]**

> Then in complete shock i got pregnant on my 1st cycle. Im still in shock and after over 3 years of heartbreak and multiple breakdowns my heart and head refuses to believe its real. Im only 6 weeks today and i can't bring myself to read any pregnancy books or listen to any audiobooks.
> 
> Every lack of symptom is scaring me despite knowing after constant googling and researching this is "normal"
> 
> I just can't bring myself to accept. Or believe or even be happy. I'm just waiting for the other shoe to drop and i feel guilty about it.
> 
> I have an early 1st scan on the 19th this month with my fertility clinic. I believe due to the letrozol miniscule chance of twins. But i dont even think that will be enough to make it real. Assuming there isnt any problems. I feel like a nervous wreck and i don't know what to do.

**[pbu11-0287]**

> After we found out we were having a boy, my husband and I were so excited and wanted to buy his first outfit and some other things but we had been so hesitant because of the people who said not to.
> 
> I’m 15 weeks on Wednesday and we just told one of my husbands cousins and she bought our boy two outfits 🥺 it was so so sweet and they have been hanging in our closet! everything is still perfectly fine! and now we feel more comfortable buying him things.
> 
> Yes, they probably just mean not to buy things too early in case of anything but… that just still feels wrong. the way I’ve always been told, it feels mean and like such a awful scare tactic. TW: >!we lost our first baby to a miscarriage last year and have already been so fearful this pregnancy, so obviously we want to avoid anything that could cause another loss but buying things for your baby would never cause a loss. I’m sad we had felt so scared to do it.!< 

**[pbu11-0341]**

> I think I sort of had a breakdown?  During a prior relationship I was actively trying and kept miscarrying.  I kept expecting this one would miscarry also.  And I went off my meds because I thought they might hurt the baby, I think that made me a little paranoid.  I just never thought I'd keep the pregnancy and kind of thought something bad would happen if I told anyone?  I have no particular reason to think that...the worst that could happen if I told my boyfriend is he might tell me to get an abortion, and I could just say "No".  (If he even said that, which is by no means certain.)
> 
> I finally realized I was spiraling and talked to my doctor about a pregnancy safe medication regime and everything is clearer.
> 
> I haven't bought anything, haven't been to the OBGYN in months, and my boyfriend doesn't know.  At this point if I tell him I'll seem crazy, and he'll be hurt.

**[pbu02-0006]**

> I am terrified to get an ultrasound again out of fear of being told there’s no heartbeat again I truly don’t know what to do, I thought if I got pregnant again I’d get an early private scan but the fear was too strong I held off.

**[pbu02-0025]**

> I've noticed that it's triggering me quite a fair amount being bombarded constantly about baby showers and gender reveals and people already buying things for my baby when I haven't even begun buying anything because my anxiety and trauma from the previous losses has me on edge. I dont want to celebrate anything, I dont want baby showers or gender reveals because I'm constantly thinking that anything could go wrong and I won't be able to breathe and relax until my baby is here and I can feel their skin and hear them cry and feel them breathe.

**[pbu02-0026]**

> "please stop" the reply was "awwww I know but I just can't help myself".

**[pbu02-0042]**

> Last week I suffered a miscarriage at 6+6. I‘m pretty devastated and I now have all of these clothes that I bought and won’t be able to use. I really need to get them out of my closet because they are too hard to look at now and are associated with my loss.

**[pbu02-0130]**

> This is my 5th pregnancy after a long trajectory of infertility treatment and I just feel my rainbow baby might slip away any moment.
> 
> I also bought a little onesie to commemorate reaching the second trimester and I feel I jinxed everything.🙈

**[pbu02-0271]**

> I don’t know when to start shopping. I know the earlier the better, but what if I end up going through a miscarriage again? I’ll have to look at everything until I can return it all or give it away.

**[pbu05-0334]**

> I’m currently TTC # 1 after a MMC and I’d love to get a recommendation on a pregnancy journal to track feelings and exciting experiences during my next pregnancy. Thank you 💕

**Further supporting IDs:** None beyond the passages above.

### 6. Wanting a child, loving the baby, regretting pregnancy and considering abortion can coexist; emotions and decisions can change without following one predictable path.

**Current findings section:** §1 audience; §2 stage arc; §6 identity and shame; §9 segments; §10 product premise.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends and contradicts the implied settled-continuation premise.**

Current wording: “She is pregnant, often for the first time”

**What should change / interpretation:** Keep pregnancy as the shared situation, but stop assuming all readers are settled on continuation or that planned conception, IVF, prior caregiving or a supportive partner predicts excitement. Distinguish an unwanted thought, a wish for the suffering to stop, an active decision, anticipated regret, actual grief and later relief. Do not promise that either continuation or termination produces a particular feeling.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0053]**

> Eventually, two years after my surgery, I agreed to attempt an embryo transfer. I did this purely out of deep love for my husband, though I was never wholeheartedly invested in becoming a mother. We explicitly agreed that if the process succeeded, he would take on 80% of the parenting responsibilities.

**[pbu11-0054]**

> ​In your experience, could my inability to connect with the pregnancy—and this overwhelming, dark feeling of being trapped by motherhood—be exacerbated by discontinuing sertraline, or is it fundamentally because I truly do not want a child?
> 
> ​I am currently in therapy, but it hasn't been very helpful because my goals and my husband's needs are completely at odds. I don't want to end up raising a child I didn't want only to get divorced anyway, nor do I want to get an abortion and regret it later. I feel foolish for putting someone else’s dreams ahead of my own boundaries.

**[pbu11-0200]**

> I'm 6 weeks pregnant and sometimes, especially at night when the pregnancy cramps are bad, I regret it all. I wonder if I'm strong enough, mentally and physically, to go through with this. It gets so bad I think of terminating the pregnancy, because in my state it's still legal at this stage.
> 
> In the morning I think about my thoughts from the night prior, the pattern I'm seeing, and second guess everything. Do I really want this? When I'm feeling fine, yes, I really do want this. But when I'm in the trenches it seems I just want it to **stop.** The guilt in the morning is as strong as the regret at night. 
> 
> Am I too weak to handle this? Am I absolutely crazy? My feelings are *literally* night and day... What should I do?

**[pbu11-0214]**

> I hate myself for allowing myself to get pregnant, I hate being pregnant, I hate the baby, I hate the child’s father most days and I hate it. I’m always tired. I’m never in the mood for anything. I still live with my parents and crave support from my partner that I don’t get. I turn 21 next month and can’t even celebrate how I want to. I’m ashamed of being pregnant and I’m not even married nor do I know this man well enough to want to continue being with him. I’m just all around miserable and blame no one but myself. This whole experience I’ve done nothing but cry and hate every single day.  
> Then I realize this is the rest of my life. I gotta give birth and put my life on the line for a man and child I don’t even know. I gotta feed it, clothe it, take care of it and love it for the rest of my life and it makes me miserable. I also worked hard keeping my figure and weight being I use to be overweight and it all feels like it was for nothing. 

**[pbu11-0215]**

> I wish I had gone through with the abortion I scheduled at nine weeks. Two baby daddy’s at 25 years old was not what I had planned. Not while going through a divorce, the stress alone from that is killing me. Right alongside hiding my bump every time we have to exchange my 2.5 year old now that he’s back from deployment. 

**[pbu11-0216]**

> If I could go back to never meeting my boyfriend and my soon to be ex-husband being on deployment and it just being me and my toddler I would. I miss it just being the two of us, and I can’t seem to bond with this baby nor my boyfriend because there seems to be a mental block there for me. Sometimes I don’t even feel like I love him. Sometimes I wish that at our appointments there will be no heartbeat so I can just get out of this situation entirely and go back to how it was. 

**[pbu11-0239]**

> My husband is honestly distraught over the pregnancy. He’s sick to his stomach about it and believes not going through with this is the only realistic option because of his age and the future we thought we were heading toward. To be clear, he has not been mean, angry, or unsupportive. He’s been caring and concerned, and he’s trying to support me emotionally, but he sees this very differently than I do.
> 
> I had an ultrasound yesterday, and for a brief moment there was concern that something might be wrong. The second I thought that might be the case, I completely crumbled. I cried, panicked, and felt a level of fear and sadness that honestly surprised me. That reaction forced me to confront feelings I had been trying not to think about.
> 
> Then, when everything turned out to be okay, the overwhelming sense of relief hit me just as hard. I can’t even fully explain it. In that moment, I realized how attached I had already become and how much I wanted everything to be okay. That experience made it impossible for me to keep pretending I was emotionally detached from all of this.

**[pbu11-0240]**

> What scares me is that if I don’t go through with this pregnancy, I don’t know if I’ll ever truly get over it. Right now, it feels like it would break a part of me. Because of our ages and where we are in life, I know this would almost certainly be my last chance to be a mom of two. The thought of walking away from that possibility is something I’m struggling to put into words.

**[pbu11-0245]**

> Now I’m 25, almost 26 weeks pregnant with my husband’s baby. My life has changed for the better, and I’m definitely in a much better place than I was back then.
> 
> Sometimes when I see pro-life TikToks or ads, they make me feel uncomfortable because they seem designed to make women feel guilty. That’s never really been how I’ve felt about my abortion, but seeing that kind of content can still be unsettling.
> 
> Has anyone else experienced something similar?

**[pbu11-0357]**

> I know it’s not the baby’s fault, they didn’t do anything wrong. They’re just trying to grow. They’re just developing normally. I almost blame them for everything. I feel so horrible for thinking that everything would be better if I wasn’t pregnant, sometimes I wish I would miscarry just to feel relief and I cry so much afterwards because it’s not what I really feel. I have love for this baby, it’s just so hard to feel it when I’m going through such a bad time. 

**[pbu11-0358]**

> I’m 13 weeks pregnant and I don’t really know how to explain this without sounding ungrateful or like I don’t want my baby, because I do. I saw my baby today on the ultrasound and for a moment I felt something. I felt emotional and connected and like I was actually there. And then I came home and it was like I went right back to being numb.

**[pbu11-0359]**

> And sometimes I have thoughts that scare me. Sometimes I wish I would miscarry. That feels so fucked up to say because I don’t want this baby to have a mother like me. I don’t want to hurt my baby, and I don’t want to lose my baby, but I also feel overwhelmed, numb, and afraid of becoming a mother when I barely feel present in my own life. I feel ashamed for even thinking it.
> 
> And it’s destroying my marriage.
> 
> My husband feels emotionally neglected by me, and I understand why. From his perspective, I’m distant and withdrawn and I’m not giving him the affection or emotional connection he needs from his wife. It has made our marriage miserable lately because he feels unwanted and I feel constantly guilty for not being able to give him something that I don’t even feel like I have inside of me right now.

**[pbu02-0013]**

> Started waking up with what I can only describe as impending doom, complete and utter entrapment in my head. It felt / still feels as if my life is a long, linear corridor with just one door at the end to get out and the walls are getting thinner and thinner, closing in on me. When my partner and I originally spoke about things, I said I feel like he’ll leave me if I have a termination. He said “he doesn’t know what would happen” which made me feel even more pushed to one choice.

**[pbu02-0014]**

> My mental health improved slightly when I confessed to my partner I’m not sure what I want anymore & the second that a termination became an option, I felt like I could breathe again.

**[pbu02-0033]**

> My plan is to end this pregnancy now, and try again when I'm actually standing on solid ground, insurance sorted, my own mind quieter, some real certainty about where we'll even be living. Not because I don't want this life with him. Because I do. Just not stitched together out of fear and bad timing.

**[pbu02-0043]**

> I know this might sound selfish, but for the first time in my adult life, I was starting to see a version of myself outside of motherhood. My kids are getting older. They need me differently now. I’ve recently started learning how to love myself, figuring out who I am as a person, discovering things I enjoy, and actually thinking about what I want my life to look like.

**[pbu02-0198]**

> Completely depressed, tired, anxious, fearful, scared to go through pregnancy and birth (even though I had a great pregnancy last time), I don’t understand how a wanted and planned pregnancy could feel so crappy. I have massive guilt over the way I feel seeing how happy my husband is & I fear I will feel this way the whole pregnancy… it’s like it already changed my identity and I get in bouts of panic like idk if I can do this. (for the record I am on Zoloft and was with all my other pregnancies for anxiety/depression and had no issues but this time it’s almost like I got pregnant and it stopped working).

**[pbu02-0274]**

> My brain kept throwing thoughts at me like, *“What if I don’t want this?” “What if I regret this?” “What if I never love my baby?” “What if I’ve made a terrible mistake?”* I desperately looked for an escape route because everything felt so overwhelming. At one point, I even wanted to stop my IVF medication and end the pregnancy.

**[pbu02-0275]**

> I don’t think I got here by forcing myself to feel excited or constantly checking whether I “loved” my pregnancy enough. It happened gradually, while I worked on my anxiety and learned not to treat every frightening thought as a statement of what I truly wanted.

**[pbu02-0281]**

> I am no longer excited, i no longer even want to imagine my life after. I don’t want life to change anymore. i feel like an awful mother to this unborn child already. i have awful thoughts of hoping maybe i will miscarry (i don’t believe that’s something i genuinely want to happen and will not act on these thoughts to cause harm to myself or my child). is this normal? i don’t necessarily feel depressed, more or not i feel lonely. not having my partner with me through these first few months is hurting my heart and i feel like it’s possible that’s the reason i don’t feel connected to this baby or the future to come.

**[pbu02-0312]**

> Lately, I’ve been wishing something would happen to me like a car crash, random attack, fall, so that my pregnancy would lead to miscarriage. Every time I go to the toilet or shower I’m hoping to find blood so that it’s not ‘my’ fault.   
> I’m not at a point where I would harm myself, but I can’t shake these thoughts from my brain.   
> What if I end up just like my father did, as he didn’t look after his mental health before having kids, and ended up a sour, alone man who had no relationship with his kids.   
> What if I have this baby, and these feelings don’t get better?   
> Am I better off just terminating this pregnancy, getting my mental health in order, then trying again?

**[pbu02-0321]**

> This whole pregnancy I haven’t felt happy or excited about anything baby related. I feel like I’ve been numb this whole pregnancy. I don’t have a support system other than my husband. I am no contact with my parents. I have felt so alone and depressed throughout this whole pregnancy. Today I haven’t been able to stop crying because I feel like the worse person in the world because I’m still not happy about this pregnancy. I feel like I’m going to be a terrible mom even though I’ve been great with kids my whole life and everyone has told me I would make such a great mom. All I can think about is how much I regret getting pregnant and keeping the pregnancy.

**[pbu05-0091]**

> We currently have a 9 month old and did not planning on having anymore, but somehow my birth control failed…   
> I will be getting my tubes tied this time around. I’m honestly very scared and filled with a ton of anxiety because we just got to a very good place with our son. 
> 
> I want to start out by saying that my mental health was absolutely horrible when we had our first baby, he was a very hard baby up until about 5 months and I struggled a lot… maybe more than others sadly… I was filled with horrible regret, depression, and overall I hated my life and choices. Obviously I don’t feel as awful now, and my head space is MUCH better these days. I think it’s because my son is so much easier now and doesn’t scream all night and day haha. He’s much more enjoyable and I find things to be incredibly easy compared to those newborn days. Those who had a hard time mentally, did you find it worse the second time around?

**[pbu05-0092]**

> I’m just very scared for my mental health and I truly didn’t want a second child. I find things to be manageable with one, and I’m worried a second will rip my life apart even more. I also want to state that I didn’t want to revolve my life around raising children…. I very much enjoy other things in life.  I still don’t find being a caregiver amazing or my whole life’s purpose. What did you do to prepare yourself mentally for the newborn phase again, and what steps should I take to make sure I don’t lose my mind?

**[pbu05-0133]**

> My fiancé and I are getting married this October, and we have a 4 year old daughter who honestly completed our little family in our eyes. We were very content being one and done. We had fully accepted that stage of life and were excited for what was ahead. So finding out I’m pregnant has been a lot.
> 
> This was absolutely not expected, and I feel like I’m grieving the life and plans I thought were set. I know that probably sounds awful to say out loud, but I want to be honest because I can’t be the only person who has felt this way.
> 
> I’m very much a “her body, her choice” person, and for me personally, I’m choosing to continue the pregnancy. But I’d be lying if I said I’m excited right now. I’m scared, overwhelmed, emotional, and honestly not looking forward to pregnancy or starting over again after finally getting some independence back with our daughter being 4.
> 
> At the same time, deep down, I know one day I’ll look at this child and wonder how our family ever existed without them. I know this baby will be loved so much. It’s just hard to see past the shock and fear right now.
> 
> I guess I’m just looking to hear from other people who were happily one and done until suddenly they weren’t, or any oopsie babies that brought you joy!

**[pbu05-0258]**

> It’s funny because I’m 5 months pregnant now and I’ve felt myself hyper fixating on emotions, like if I feel something good I’m so afraid it’ll escape me- and if I feel something bad it’s just proof I’m having an unhappy pregnancy. sometimes when I look at social media or talk to other pregnant people, it’s as if people get put into two pregnancy categories; happy pregnancies and unhappy pregnancies (albeit complications are scary and hard and I don’t want to undermine that) 
> 
> But I don’t know, i feel a lot of pressure to have a totally happy pregnancy, and sad/uncomfortable emotions are a failure. But it’s 9 months! Im not gonna feel happy the whole time. Im gonna have a range of emotions, like I have every other period of my life.   
> I remembered that advice I got for my acid trip and it’s really helped. I guess with the drug your neurons are all distorted and misfiring, and pregnancy your hormones are all over the place too too- but I’m still going through my range of emotions, just with a new lens

**[pbu05-0282]**

>

I think we are both pretty scared and anxious. I’ve talked to my mother as well and she pretty anti abortion so she’s telling me to keep it obvi (she’s more excited than I am)
>

>

>

> 
> 5. We don’t know if we are financially or emotionally prepared to have a child just yet. While we both have talked about it and most definitely want children in the near future, we just don’t know.

I got lucky to get a government city job, but the downside is it’s only casual so I have no benefits and work is not garunteed. I kinda want to go to school and get a stable career in line before I have kids, which is another thing in weighing, too.

### 7. Money, insurance, housing, immigration and service delays can change whether a pregnancy continues and which choices still feel available.

**Current findings section:** §8 care costs and insurance; §10 limits of a guide; §13 geographic gaps.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends.**

Current wording: “The money stress is mostly bills, daycare and leave”

**What should change / interpretation:** Add the cost and timing of continuation, abortion and urgent food needs. Insurance cancellation and repeat appointments can move gestation forward while feelings change. Cross-border residence, lack of transport, reported legal restrictions and housing dependence are constraints, not tasks solved by a shopping list. These are accounts of access and perceived options, not verified statements about local law.

### 8. Partner problems include reproductive coercion, threats, physical violence, sexual pressure and control of housing or information, in both directions of the pregnancy decision.

**Current findings section:** §5 partners; §6 visitors and bodily control; §10 partner section.

**Relationship to current conclusion: contradicts the remaining mental-load-first generalization and extends the restoration.**

Current wording: “From her side, the gap is mental load more than absence:”

**What should change / interpretation:** Replace this broad characterization with distinct situations: unequal tasks, disagreement, abandonment, coercion and violence. A short partner task list addresses only some of them. Pressure can seek abortion, continuation, another pregnancy or sex before recovery. Preserve descriptions of caring disagreement separately from threats; a partner's wish to be involved does not establish consent or safe involvement.

### 9. Asking for help can feel like risking custody, judgment or the birth a parent has prepared for, which can keep serious needs undisclosed.

**Current findings section:** §5 professionals; §6 fear/shame; §10 care navigation.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends.**

Current wording: “Care navigation includes trust and being believed.”

**What should change / interpretation:** Add fear of the consequences of disclosure: self-harm scars, substance use, old medical history, medication, psychiatric symptoms and reported harm to a baby. Knowing whom to contact is insufficient when asking feels dangerous. Distinguish anticipated consequences, a reported CPS referral, and a parent who sought treatment despite a custody threat; none establishes reporting rules.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0069]**

> When I was assessed for PPD and was asking for help because I felt down when I wasn’t normally down, I was reported to CPS and it petrified me, I don’t want to talk to a professional ever again.

> Ive tried many medications, and i am now stable on the perfect med combo for me. Took many tears, so much effort, and so much vulnerability. But im here. I'm stable and a great mom. I'm quick to call my psychiatrist or her office for any weird symptoms. I dont miss my meds for anything. When ive needed medical care, I get it. Post partum, I had my ex take our baby while I got treatment. He threatened to use it to remove custody from me, although he never ended up doing so, but i went to the hospital anyways and was honest with him about the PPD (later confirmed PPP). He didnt end up using it to take custody from me, but i fully thought he would and I put my baby first anyways. The doctors never called CPS on me, but my thoughts never escalated beyond wanting to hurt myself.

### 10. Perinatal mental distress includes suicidal thoughts, delusions, intrusive thoughts and reported actions toward babies, alongside accounts of treatment and recovery.

**Current findings section:** §2 first weeks; §5 partner needs; §6 fear and shame.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends.**

Current wording: “The first weeks include incapacity, feeding pressure and delayed connection.”

**What should change / interpretation:** Name the scenes that the broad phrase does not convey. Keep intrusive thoughts, stated lack of intent, delusional beliefs and actual reported actions separate. Include fathers' experiences and treatment outcomes; do not infer a diagnosis from a post or reduce these accounts to ordinary baby blues.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0121]**

> In 2021, my husband and I decided to try for a baby. After a year of heartbreak, we finally got our yes. But instead of joy, I felt dread. My family weren’t very supportive, and my workplace wasn’t either. Around 5 months pregnant, I developed antenatal depression. I hated everything: my life, my husband (who I’d always been inseparable from), everything around me.
> 
> Our son was born after 5 days of a failed induction, ending in an emergency c-section. I felt nothing. Completely numb.

**[pbu11-0122]**

> But slowly, things started to change. Around a year and a half in, I began to improve. I left my job, and life became lighter. By the time my son was two, everything shifted. My bond with him came. My maternal instincts kicked in. Now, he’s my best friend. My relationship with my husband healed too, we’re closer than ever.

**[pbu11-0123]**

> A month later, I fell pregnant again. And now I’m here with my 4-week-old baby boy.
> 
> This time, it’s completely different. I adore my boys. I love being a mum. I’m present. I’m stable. I get to enjoy this.

**[pbu11-0133]**

> i was taking my medication during my pregnancy but i was unfortunately hospitalized my 3rd month of pregnancy for 3 days. my ob called me frantically asking if i felt okay. i said yes??? why???? apparently on some blood work my glucose was almost in the 600s and she told me to go to the er right then. so i did, my fiance rushed me to the er, i explained what happened and i was there for 3 days just to be told i have type 2 diabetes. i was taking 3 different medications just for anxiety and depression. i was taking buspirone, zoloft & vraylar. the doctor came in and basically told me i needed to stop vraylar cold turkey cause they thought it was the culprit for my high glucose. my heart kinda stopped when he said that because vraylar was my saving grace. it was the only thing that kept my meds working and that was the first and only combination of meds that actually helped me and continued to help me as i unfortunately have issues when it comes to medication. (every other medication i tried in the past would only work for a few months then it would completely stop, it’s like i was taking a sugar pill) i was also taking 100mg of zoloft. being at the hospital for days, threw me off my routine for my medications (which im already terrible at) so unfortunately when i got back home, i tried to get back on track with taking my other mental meds but it wasn’t happening so i basically said f this and just stopped all medications i was on. now, i knew i would struggle and i knew it would get back and it did. i wanted to die, i slept and slept and cried while growing my baby girl. i got through it, and today i am still unmediated but i don’t feel suicidal and haven’t been since january of this year.

**[pbu11-0135]**

> lately, i get overwhelmed, overstimulated, i see red. i see red and i stop everything im going and i gently toss her in front of me onto the bed. i don’t throw her, i don’t slam her, her head does not bounce off the bed, she doesn’t bounce multiple times. i would say it’s like tossing your phone onto the bed, it’s gentle but it’s not even a foot in front of me and isn’t getting tossed above my chest. i would say it at or below chest level when i have done this. it’s happened maybe 3 times when i just switch and have to get her away. she still cries but she doesn’t cry harder or show any signs of being hurt? it doesn’t help my guilt though. once it happened and i do the initial action, my anger leaves. it completely leaves and i feel okay again but im left feeling terrible. i feel guilty and i wonder if she’ll remember or if she hates me or if im just a bad mom. it happens and then i’m left feeling worse than before, for hours.

**[pbu11-0136]**

> i’ve unfortunately also placed pillows on her. that one makes my chest ache. i know it’s not right. i know it’s awful. i don’t press the pillow, i don’t smother her and i once again don’t walk off or leave the room. it’s happened twice, both times it was on her for maybe 10 seconds then afterwards i calmly move it, pick her up, and console/soothe her while in tears because like ive said many times. i feel awful. i feel dirty. i feel like im a terrible mother and don’t deserve my daughter. i don’t want to hurt her, id never want to actually hurt or harm my daughter in any way. she is wanted and she is loved. that’s not a problem or something that’s ever crossed my mind. in all situations she has been fine. she does stop crying and i do hold her, apologize and kiss her for hours on end because i don’t know what else to do afterwards.

**[pbu11-0373]**

> It felt like at that time, I was either going to commit suicide or smoke—it was the only thing that calmed me down. One day, while sitting down, I had a crisis; there was a completely full pack of cigarettes in front of me, and I smoked the entire pack back-to-back without any break. In other words, I chain-smoked 20 cigarettes sequentially without stopping. That day, I went to the hospital first, and then to a psychiatrist. Now, I am far away from those thoughts, and according to the doctors, the baby is perfectly healthy. I am exactly 24 weeks pregnant, and it appears as 26 weeks on the ultrasound. (I haven't smoked a single cigarette since the 14th week). However, my mind and conscience are constantly stuck on those first 14 weeks. What if I caused permanent damage to the baby? I need some positive stories about this. Because smoking without a break like that was a mild carbon monoxide poisoning, not to mention taking all those toxins at once.. **feel like I'm the only cruel person who has ever done this and that I have caused irreversible damage to the baby.**

I want my husband to be okay. My mother AND grandmother passed away during my second pregnancy. I have no one to lean on. Can I lean on y'all? **[pbu05-0164]**

**[pbu05-0397]**

> I was super depressed, I would get panic attacks and I couldn’t take care of myself. I couldn’t eat etc. My husband really encouraged me to look into antidepressants because it was getting that bad. 
>

The medication really did work amazingly for me. I didn’t get very many negative side effects to my knowledge, and I was seeing a huge shift in my anxiety and OCD spirals. I felt a difference almost immediately, and I increased because it would work for awhile and then I’d start getting the same symptoms creeping back, and my doctors said I probably just needed a higher dose.

**[pbu05-0398]**

I have nightmares about the baby, I’m anxious about him being here. I’m not suicidal at all which is great, I think the medicine has helped a lot with that, but this just sucks. 
>

### 11. Good partners, extensive support and advance mental-health preparation can coexist with severe distress; getting treatment can require more than encouragement.

**Current findings section:** §5 support/professionals; §10 guide limits; §17.4 help plan.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends; limits what task-based support can explain.**

Current wording: “Give the partner a short task list in a non-reading format”

**What should change / interpretation:** Keep useful tasks, but do not imply that enough tasks, leave or preparation prevents a crisis. One mother has therapy, psychiatry, medication planning, three months of paternal leave and family/neighbors, yet cannot cope. Others need an insured prescriber, an appointment or actual respite, rather than more baby-care resources. A partner can help and still not resolve symptoms.

**Verbatim evidence:**

**[pbu11-0064]**

> We tried to thoroughly prepare for the possibility of PPD. I'm still in therapy, I see a psychiatrist regularly, my team increased my medication dosage in preparation for the difficulty of postpartum, I have countless people who have told me they are willing to watch our children or bring meals or help however they can. My husband reminds me to use their proffered support, he tries to give me breaks and space and support and peace. I have been on Zoloft for three years, during the pregnancy of our first, then added Wellbutrin shortly after to try to combat the completely-wiped-out sex drive.

**[pbu11-0065]**

> I kept quiet about how much I felt like I was drowning for weeks, hoping it was simply the adjustment period that would go away on its own. I am privileged in how much support I have: free military health care, three months paid paternity leave for my husband, family that flew out to Alaska to help in the first couple weeks postpartum, support from neighbors and friends, and more. We just got back from a two week visit to family and when I still felt unable to cope or find much enjoyment in this time that should be more restful with 24/7 help, I finally admitted to my husband the depth of what I'm feeling.

**[pbu11-0284]**

> I have no doubt I would be a great parent, however I am so worried about post natal depression and anxiety. I will definitely get it. I have had panic disorders all my life and I know when one comes up I am stuck in bed unable to move. Last year when one came on I had to get my mum to come from interstate to be with me because I was just petrified to leave the house (woo hoo panic disorder!). 
> 
> Im just thinking about this pregnancy and worried I won’t be able to provide the support a baby needs. My partner will be there so support of course but anxiety is a dark place and often people can’t really help. 

**[pbu11-0330]**

I finally have felt well enough to work out again. I went on a little jog which usually does the trick when I’m sad, but I got NO ENDORPHINS from it. I’ve felt so detached from myself and my body these last few weeks and I am so depressed. It’s gotten to the point where I am numb even during sex. I keep asking my husband for help and he isn’t understanding. I know this is confusing to him because he does everything for us. Cooks, cleans, built out bed and the crib. I see him trying so hard and I am still struggling. I feel like I’m drowning. I don’t understand how I am supposed to do 6 more months of this. I don’t have an inclining of a nesting instinct and I don’t feel attached to my baby at all. All I feel is this growing resentment. At this point every time my husband addresses me it’s with a sigh and exhaustion. I feel like my life is crumbling. I’m miserable and I miss the feeling of joy.

### 12. Medication decisions concern a parent's ability to function as well as fear of harming the baby; stopping, restarting and side effects complicate reassurance.

**Current findings section:** §2 symptoms; §4 remedies; §5 care; §6 guilt.

**Relationship to current conclusion: extends.**

Current wording: “The broken week-by-week script includes persistent pain, exhaustion, vomiting and incontinence, repeated remedies and disrupted sleep.”

**What should change / interpretation:** Add previous medication response, abrupt cessation after an insurance gap or pregnancy discovery, fear of a new prescription, uncertainty after clinician advice, and functioning restored or worsened. Retain authors' claims as claims; the evidence does not establish comparative safety, dosage or efficacy. Include prescribed-treatment fear alongside avoidance of treatment after an adverse experience.

### 13. Symptoms can stop eating, sleeping, walking, working or caring for another child, and generic reassurance or common remedies may not meet the immediate need.

**Current findings section:** §2 symptoms and daily function; §3 stock lines; §4 attempts at relief; §6 body.

**Relationship to current conclusion: confirms and extends.**

Current wording: “The broken week-by-week script includes persistent pain, exhaustion, vomiting and incontinence, repeated remedies and disrupted sleep.”

**What should change / interpretation:** The restoration is supported. Add interacting conditions, failed treatment, disability and sensory overload, the demand for relief now, and eating-disorder recovery. Positive treatment outcomes belong beside failures. Symptoms can bring thoughts of termination or resentment without establishing lack of love for the child.
